267 ilustrasi tambahan Mythics

267/267 lolos audit dan terpasang. Semua karakter dipilih dari entri dengan informasi lengkap. Ilustrasi adalah interpretasi artistik AI berdasarkan sumber riset.

Penanggalan

Nocturnal floating head with spectral non-graphic organ forms

Aura: malevolent nocturnal predatory horror

Audit ciri dan sumber
  • Floating woman's head without torso or limbs
  • Spectral organ cluster and coils descending directly from neck
  • Frightening facial expression and sinister night aura

Observed isolated floating female head and neck, tangled dark hair, no torso or limbs. Purple spectral organ cluster and recognizable intestine coils descend directly from neck. Severe gaunt face, staring pale eyes, angry brow and grimace create frightening malevolent aura in moonlit stilt-house setting. Spectral variant avoids graphic injury.

Review sumber
One square dark painterly illustration of Malay Penanggalan folklore, suitable for a mythological encyclopedia. The subject is a floating adult woman's HEAD ONLY, long tangled black hair blowing wildly, absolutely no human body or limbs. Beneath her neck, semi-transparent purple spectral forms evoke the traditional dangling organ cluster and coils through ethereal mist; these are ghostly light, NOT solid biological tissue and NOT flat paper or ribbons. Show the full hovering head and descending ghostly forms clearly against a dark misty tropical night sky; distant Malay stilt house small behind her. Her face must be frightening: severe gaunt features, unnatural fixed wide-eyed stare, angry eyebrows, and a grimacing mouth. Use stark upward moonlight and unsettling shadows. No beauty portrait, no glamorous makeup, no alluring expression. Atmospheric supernatural horror, restrained and non-graphic, no injuries, blood, bodily fluids or victims. Comfortable margin all around full subject. No wings, horns, weapons, text or watermark.

The head is NOT a jackal, dog, wolf, fox, horse, or ordinary donkey. Dark reddish animal head, muscular human torso and human limbs, simple ancient Egyptian linen kilt and broad collar. Hands empty.
